> **▶ 2026-08-19 · `auth_recovery_flow` — READY_FOR_SELF_REVIEW.** Password reset actually works now. Before this, a reset link **silently signed the player in with the password unchanged**: `AuthService.OnDeepLink` had one branch, `type=recovery` in the fragment was never read, and the recovery tokens were applied as an ordinary session. Cowork wrote the C# half (uncommitted at handoff): `OAuthCallbackParser.GetCallbackInfo` exposes `type` / `error` / `error_code` / `error_description`; `OnDeepLink` now branches three ways — `type=recovery` **holds** the tokens in `PendingRecovery` (nothing persisted, no `SignedIn`), an error fragment with no pending OAuth surfaces a localized failure, and everything else is byte-for-byte the old path; `UpdatePassword` landed on all three clients; `ResetPasswordScreenController` + 9 `AUTH_RESET_*` EN/JA rows + 14 tests. **This Code session finished it:** built `ResetPasswordScreen` in ShellScene as a clone of `LoginScreen` (27 GameObjects, every element cloned — nothing hand-built), wired all 18 `[SerializeField]`s and `ScreenManager._resetPasswordScreen`, and added `ApplyLocalization()` to the controller because **the project has no `LocalizedText` component** — the account screens carry hardcoded English in-scene and the existing `AUTH_LOGIN_*` / `AUTH_SIGNUP_*` keys have **zero consumers**. ResetPassword is the first account screen that genuinely localizes; the other four are a small follow-up. Two things worth remembering: (1) the clone inherited `LayoutElement.preferredWidth=388` baked for the word LOGIN, which overrode the button's own ContentSizeFitter and pushed `SET PASSWORD` (467px) outside the green fill — `preferredWidth=-1` hands sizing back to the CSF; (2) saving the scene **after a play-mode session** re-baked anchor churn into 154 pre-existing RectTransforms + 3 PrefabInstances (the `project_scene_save_bakes_layout_churn` scar), repaired by restoring every HEAD block except the two intentional ones, then reopening the scene. Verified in play mode through the real entry path (title gate → Login → `HandleAuthCallback`): tokens held not persisted, weak/mismatch/expired errors, Back clearing tokens first, and the plain signup-confirmation regression guard. The Editor runs the **live** transport, so a fabricated recovery token was genuinely refused by Supabase — the failure branch is proven end-to-end, the success branch needs a real emailed link. Full unfiltered EditMode sweep: **1492/1496**, the one failure (`GameSessionTests.OnHoleComplete_…`, a `[Golfin.Telemetry]` `DontDestroyOnLoad` in EditMode) logged here as "pre-existing and unrelated" — **it was neither, and it is now FIXED**; see the `beta_telemetry` entry. Nothing to do on this task. **Device-only items remain:** real iPhone mail-tap, a real token succeeding + old password rejected, EN/JA on device. **Open NOTE:** Supabase's password minimum length is still unverified against the dashboard (client enforces 8+, stricter than the default 6). Report: `Docs/Specs/Active/auth_recovery_flow/IMPLEMENTER_REPORT.md`.

> **▶ 2026-08-19 · `tournament_restrictions` (client half) — ✅ ARCHITECT_APPROVED, committed `fb44640ae`, pushed.** Tournaments now carry a category and entry restrictions. The **server half has been live in prod since 2026-08-18** and was not touched: `list_golfin` emits 10 nullable fields (`category`, `max_players`, `players_per_division`, `division_type`, `char_rarity_min/max`, `char_level_min/max`, `gear_rule`, `club_rarity_max`) and `POST /golfin/{slug}/enter` denies **before the fee debit** with 200-shaped `{status:"full"|"ineligible"}`. Client: the 10 fields ride DTO → `TournamentDefinition` (appended-optional, the `Title`/`BannerUrl` pattern) → mapper as a pass-through; **`Assets/Resources/Data/tournaments.csv` gained no columns**, so the offline path behaves exactly as before. The signup modal's RULES block is now composed **from data** (`TournamentRulesText`, pure and therefore test-gated in EN *and* JA) with the five original localization keys surviving as the null fallbacks — an unrestricted tournament renders byte-for-byte what it rendered yesterday. **One intended copy change:** `tourn.rules.gear` said "Supplied by GOLFIN", which was display fiction; it now shows only when the field is genuinely absent, and every server-fed tournament (all backfilled to `own`) reads "Own clubs". CONFIRM is gated **before the payment path** by `TournamentEligibility` — a pure evaluator taking ranks rather than managers, mirroring `_check_entry_eligibility` case for case, including its deny-when-unresolvable branches — sited *after* the already-entered short-circuit so a player already in is never thrown out by a rule they now fail. `gear_rule=supplied` skips the bag check entirely. Server `full`/`ineligible` denials map onto the same failure vocabulary and the same toast copy, so a refusal reads identically whether the client or the server noticed. **A real bug surfaced only by driving the actual CONFIRM button:** the first adapter read rarity through `CharacterManager.GetCharacterTemplate`, which is the ScriptableObject *fallback* — null plus a logged error in the shipped CSV-first configuration — so every character would have been unranked and a rarity-restricted tournament would have refused **everyone**; now CSV-first with the SO as fallback, matching `GetMaxLevel`. Rarity renders as its **coloured single letter** (C/U/R/M/L/S) through `RarityHelper` — the same letters and colours every card badge already uses, and language-neutral, so the `RARITY_*` rows are not consulted. 43 new EditMode tests; full suite **1478 / 1475 passed / 0 failed / 3 pre-existing skips**. 16 new localization rows (EN+JA, **JA flagged for native review**). **Category tag DROPPED, not deferred** (Cesar, 2026-08-19): the SPONSOR line already carries it — a tournament presented by GOLFIN is the hardcore one, and the shipped CSV already authors `GOLFIN` literally on two rows — so the modal needs no new element and no prefab surgery. `Category` is still carried end-to-end per SPEC §1 but nothing in the UI reads it; note that `category` and `sponsor_name` are independent dashboard columns and can disagree, so they are not synonyms. **Architect review APPROVED** (`ARCHITECT_REVIEW.md`, spot-checked against the working tree, not just the report) with rulings on all four open questions and **no code change required**: **Q1** the client/server level-check asymmetry is INTENDED and recorded — the server's `golfin_character_level` is a plausibility backstop, the client checks the character actually entering, both failures are soft, do NOT converge now (if it ever matters, the additive fix is optional `character_rarity`/`character_level` fields in the enter body); **Q2** `club_rarity_max` is CONFIRMED a design knob, not an integrity rule — a modified client affects only its own gear flavour, never scores or payouts; **Q3** the A2 dashboard ships with `gear_rule=supplied` DISABLED for authoring (visible, greyed, tooltip "requires the standard-spec task") so an unplayable promise cannot be authored by accident; **Q4** inert `category` ACCEPTED, with the standing note that `category` and `sponsor_name` are independent columns and must never be treated as synonyms. **Server half committed for the record** in `playlife` `9ec20d4` (migration + `list_golfin` + enter enforcement — already deployed 2026-08-18, this only records it). **Remaining before DONE:** (1) **A2 dashboard restrictions editor** (Architect, in progress); (2) then author ONE restricted tournament (rarity band + level band + max_players; NOT `gear=supplied`) and run the **live round trip** — RULES render from `list_golfin`, ineligible CONFIRM toast, server `ineligible` on a forced mismatch, `full` at cap; (3) **JA native review** of the 16 new rows; (4) **RULES pixel fit on device** for the worst line in the real body box. Spec + report + review: `Docs/Specs/Active/tournament_restrictions/`.

Mock-auth/bot sessions skip the claim (fake tokens would 401 every editor save). 9 EditMode tests (`UsernameClaimTests`, tripwire-proved); full sweep 1435/1432/0. ✅ DEPLOYED 2026-08-18 (migration applied by Cesar; `flyctl deploy` green — `PUT /user/username` 403-not-404 against a 404 control, `/health` ok). Client `93a31e8cd`, server `playlife 76ba6b0`. Device pass (second account takes a taken name → red toast; airplane mode → save blocked) still outstanding. Rename staleness closed same day: both rename paths propagate the new name into the caller's `tournament_entries` rows (never fatal), and the migration backfills all human snapshots from `profiles` — without this, a freed name could reappear as a DUPLICATE on a board the old holder had entered.

> **▶ 2026-08-18 · `telemetry_admin_panel` — ✅ DONE (Cesar approved 2026-08-18); folder moved to `Docs/Specs/Completed/`.** The seventh admin panel, live at **https://admin.golfin.world/telemetry**, is where next week's ~20-tester beta gets read: KPI cards (testers / sessions / rounds / holes / abandons amber >20% / crashes red >0), a session funnel (`session_start → Home → hole selection → round_start → hole_complete`), per-hole difficulty (OB rate red >25%, `fps_low` median red <20), shot quality with **flick reject rate as the headline card** (`flick_rejected ÷ (flick_rejected + shot_taken)` — the "do the controls work" number), a per-tester rollup (device, build, play time, unclean exits, points Δ, crashes), and a raw event explorer that pages **server-side** with `.range()`, 100/page. Read-only: no mutation anywhere, so no `lib/audit.ts`, no POST, and `checkAdmin()` is the first line of all three route handlers (probed: no cookie → **401**, signed-in non-allowlisted → **403**, admin → 200). **Zero new npm deps** — every bar is a `<div>` with a width, because a chart library would cost the Worker more than five bars are worth. Aggregation is plain TypeScript in `lib/telemetryData.ts` over a single filtered fetch capped at **10,000 rows**, and hitting the cap raises a visible `TRUNCATED` badge rather than quietly serving half an answer. Built and verified entirely against `lib/mockTelemetry.ts`, a **frozen deterministic fixture** (no `Date.now()`, no `Math.random()`, no `randomUUID()` — two calls 4s apart returned byte-identical JSON), which is why a screenshot of it is worth something. Three deliberate design calls, each documented in the report: the funnel counts a session at a stage if it reached that stage **or any later one** (independent per-stage counting makes a real funnel read as *increasing* whenever one `screen_view` is lost in a dropped batch); the range filters on **`received_at`, not `ts`** (`ts` is the tester's device clock — one wrong-dated phone would vanish from or leak into every range); and per-tester play time sums **`max(duration_s)` per session**, because `session_end` fires on every pause with `realtimeSinceStartup`, so summing every one counts the same minutes repeatedly. Tester names reuse the Users panel's lookup — `fetchUserDirectory()` was added *inside* `lib/data.ts` next to the existing `listAllAuthUsers()` + `profiles` select, rather than growing a second profiles query pattern. EN **and** JA strings for all ~70 keys (ADMIN_DASHBOARD_OPS §3.4); the JA render was checked and nothing overflows. Deployed the sanctioned way (dev server stopped first, `npm run deploy`, `✓ bundle carries no service_role key`) and **Access verified: `/` → 302 to `late-cake-f2a4.cloudflareaccess.com`, `/telemetry` and `/api/telemetry/summary` → 302 too**. `telemetry_events` already exists and is empty (`200 []`, `content-range: */0`), which is exactly the state the panel ships into: the empty-window render was exercised and every rate comes back `null` → `—`, never `NaN`, never a divide-by-zero. **One acceptance item stays open and is not a defect in the build — it needs data that does not exist yet:** SPEC §5.6, matching the KPI totals against hand-run SQL in the Supabase editor, which can only be run once the testers have actually played. Do it during the beta week. Spec + report + captured evidence (6 API responses + 2 probe transcripts): `Docs/Specs/Completed/telemetry_admin_panel/`. **Same session, Cesar's follow-up:** the sidebar is now **alphabetical** — and sorted by the *translated* title in `app/(panels)/layout.tsx`, not by the registry array, so it reads alphabetically in English *and* in proper kana/kanji collation in Japanese (`お知らせ · テレメトリ · トーナメント · バナー · ポイント · ユーザー · 監査ログ`) instead of being alphabetical in one language and arbitrary in the other. `ADMIN_DASHBOARD_OPS.md` §1 now says seven panels and records that array order is not load-bearing; §3.1 names Telemetry as the **read-only** panel to copy (no editor, no mutation route, no `lib/audit.ts`).
>
> **▶ 2026-08-19 · `beta_telemetry` — ✅ LIVE END TO END + one post-ship defect found and fixed. Migration applied, API deployed, all 10 automated acceptance checks pass against the real endpoint. Only on-device sign-off (§5.11) is left.** **The defect is worth reading:** `auth_recovery_flow`'s sweep hit `GameSessionTests.OnHoleComplete_…` dying on a `[Golfin.Telemetry]` `DontDestroyOnLoad` and logged it as "pre-existing and unrelated" — it was MINE (that is the GameObject name in `TelemetryBehaviour`) and it **threw out of `GameSession.MarkHoleComplete` into gameplay**, violating the one invariant this feature must not break. Cause: `TelemetryHooks` subscribes to GameSession's STATIC events, which outlive play mode until the next domain reload, so an EditMode test after any play-mode session lands in the handler and calls `DontDestroyOnLoad` in edit mode. My original green sweep passed only because that Editor session had never entered play mode — order-dependent on something invisible. Fixed three ways: `TelemetryBehaviour.Instance` returns **null** outside play mode; every handler body now runs inside a `Guard()` (`isPlaying` + try/catch) because **`RecordSafe` only wrapped the payload BUILDER, not the statements around it**; and `Install()` early-returns before setting `_installed`, which was a latent bug that would have silently blocked the real play-mode install. Full EditMode suite **1494/1497 pass, 0 fail, 3 pre-existing skips**; new regression test pins the root cause.  Spec + report: `Docs/Specs/Active/beta_telemetry/`; STATUS `READY_FOR_ARCHITECT_REVIEW`. This is what tells us, from the ~20 external testers' own devices, whether the shot controls actually work (flick rejects / cancels / OB / strokes-vs-par), where testers stop (screen funnel + round abandons), and whether it is broken on their hardware (exceptions, boot→Home time, FPS). Shipped in the mandated order (ADMIN_DASHBOARD_OPS §3.2): migration → verify → deploy. Verification was by NAME, not vibes — all 13 columns probed individually with a `bogus_col` tripwire proving the check could fail, and RLS proven HOLDING (anon read `200 []`, anon insert `401 42501`), not merely enabled. Live results: `accepted:2/duplicates:0` then a replay of the identical body → `accepted:0/duplicates:2` at **200, not 500**; 101 events → 413 while exactly 100 → 200; no token → 403, malformed/bad-signature JWT → 401; zero tracebacks in `fly logs`. **A `user_id` in the request body is ignored** — a batch spoofing one stored the token's id instead, which is the security property the endpoint rests on. All 104 test rows were deleted afterwards (table verified empty) so the beta dataset starts clean. Client: new `Assets/Scripts/Telemetry/` (`Golfin.Telemetry` asmdef — the core is its own assembly because a test asmdef cannot reference Assembly-CSharp, which would have left SPEC §5 untestable) + `Assets/Scripts/TelemetryRuntime/TelemetryHooks.cs` for the Assembly-CSharp glue. `TelemetryService` is a plain C# singleton like `ApiClient`: queue → flush at 20 events / 30s / pause / quit, 500-event drop-oldest cap, exactly ONE re-enqueue on failure (safe because `event_id` is a client GUID with a unique index). **No new retry/auth/envelope layer** — `ApiClient` already does Bearer, `{data:}` unwrap, transient retry and 401-refresh-replay. **Only 5 lines were inserted into existing files**: `Endpoints.cs` (+1 URL), `ShotController.cs` (+2 event raises), `GameSession.cs` (+2 raises); everything else is new files. **12 of 13 events ship. `sp_allocated` was SKIPPED and flagged** per SPEC §1 #13 — SP allocation is unwired in the client (`CharacterManager.Awake` builds a `ManualSPAllocation` and nothing ever calls `AllocateSP`; `ConfirmPendingSP()` has no callers), so there is no commit call site to hook. Other NOTEs resolved: par = `HoleContext.Par`; build number = `AppVersion.BuildNumber` (not gated by `GOLFIN_TESTBUILD`); JSON = Newtonsoft, which Assembly-CSharp already uses in `BannersRuntime/BannerService.cs`, so no hand-rolled writer was needed; boot = `[RuntimeInitializeOnLoadMethod(AfterSceneLoad)]`, the house pattern, so **no scene edit and no prefab**. `ResetSession()` turned out to have ZERO production call sites (tests only), so `round_abandoned` keys off `ScreenChanged` as the spec's primary design said. `round_start` fires from `SetCurrentHole` too, not just `SeedSession` — PLAY NEXT starts every hole after the first, and hooking only `SeedSession` would have emitted one `round_start` per session and left `RoundActive` stale. **17/17 new EditMode tests pass; full EditMode suite 1334 passed / 0 failed / 3 pre-existing skips.** Telemetry can never break gameplay: every hook body runs inside `RecordSafe`, which builds the payload inside its own try/catch. Only §5.11 (play a hole on a real device signed in, confirm rows) is left. Admin panel is a separate spec (`telemetry_admin_panel`), NOT built here.

> **▶ 2026-08-18 · `home_notices` — ✅ DONE. Cesar verified it himself and approved; folder moved to `Docs/Specs/Completed/`. The Home notice panel is now operator-controlled — it previously told every player the servers go down on a date eight months in the past, baked into the build.** Spec + report: `Docs/Specs/Active/home_notices/`; STATUS `READY_FOR_ARCHITECT_REVIEW`. Server side was already live (`GET /api/v1/notices`, no auth, EN+JA, scheduled, ordered, capped at 5). New `Assets/Scripts/NoticesRuntime/` mirrors `BannersRuntime/` file-for-file — `RemoteNoticeDtos` / `RemoteNoticeSource` (`home_notices.json`, atomic `.tmp`+replace, RAW body cached before mapping, null on any failure) / `NoticeService` (singleton, cache read synchronously in `Awake`, then `Refresh()` off the critical path behind the same 60 s `ScheduleRefreshThrottle`). Elsewhere the diff is deliberately tiny: one `Endpoints.Notices` line, one `newsPanelRoot` field on `HomeScreenController`, and a rewritten `UpdateNewsContent`. **The DemoGate path is untouched.** **An empty notice list is a NORMAL state and hides the panel** — the bundled `HOME_MAINTENANCE_*` strings are deliberately NOT kept as an offline fallback, because a cold airplane-mode launch would then re-show the stale date this whole feature exists to remove (SPEC §4.3); they are marked unused in `LocalizationText.csv` and left in place. **19/19 new EditMode tests pass** (`NoticeResolutionTests.cs`: the language ladder, per-FIELD JA→EN fallback, EN players never falling into JA, exclusive expiry, enveloped-vs-bare parsing, malformed-JSON-changes-nothing). **One real bug found and closed while building:** `HomeScreen` is ACTIVE in `ShellScene` at load, so its `OnEnable` can run before `NoticeService.Awake` — it would then read a null `Instance`, hide the panel, and never hear about the disk cache, because a later fetch returning the SAME notices raises no event. `Awake` now raises `OnNoticesChanged` after a successful cache load (a no-op if nobody has subscribed yet), a deliberate divergence from `BannerService`, which can afford to stay silent because a banner slot that misses the boot event still shows its authored sprite. **Scene-save churn was caught and discarded:** the first `SaveScene` produced 1298/1284 lines of anchor churn; the file was restored to HEAD and only the intended hunks re-applied, so `ShellScene.unity` is **+21 / −7**, every line intentional, zero `m_IsActive`/`sizeDelta` changes. **TMP auto-size added on both notice labels** (title 24–51, body 18–39, `overflowMode=Ellipsis` as the hard backstop below the floor) so arbitrary operator copy can never draw outside the panel — ranges MEASURED against long EN+JA copy, not guessed, and the authored size is the CEILING so a short notice cannot balloon. **Then Cesar caught it still spilling sideways, and he was right:** the first check compared glyphs to each label's own RectTransform, which is NOT the box a player sees — the drawn card is `NoticePanel`'s `Notice Background` image (world x 76→1094), and the authored rects sit at 77→1077 and 77→1089, i.e. **1px of clearance on the left**, because the panel's VLG padding is `left=1, right=0` against children authored to fill the full width. Fixed with TMP `margin` insetting the text to **42px from each card edge** (the inset the card already uses vertically), which changes ZERO geometry — no `sizeDelta`, no `anchoredPosition`. Re-verified with world-space glyph extents vs the CARD: worst-case clearance across long EN, long JA and a 2000-char body is **42.8px**. Lesson: for 'text inside the box', the reference frame is the DRAWN background, never the text's own rect. **This also fixed a pre-existing clipping bug:** the title was authored at 51 in a 64px box, but `MAINTENANCE NOTICE` needs ≤49 and `メンテナンス情報` needs ≤44 — the shipped title has been clipping in both languages, worse in Japanese. **Sign-off:** Cesar checked the end-to-end behaviour himself (the §5 items that need a device plus a notice published from the dashboard) and approved. **One loose end for whoever is next in this repo:** re-run `Golfin.TournamentsRuntime.Tests` through the sanctioned runner — another live session held it ("another test run is already in progress") at close, so the 19 passes are recorded by direct fixture invocation plus one full-suite run, not by a final filtered run.

One command takes a committed tree to a TestFlight build, unattended: `./Tools/testflight.sh`.** Spec + report: `Docs/Specs/Active/fastlane_testflight_pipeline/`; STATUS `READY_FOR_ARCHITECT_REVIEW`. **Two real runs today:** `1.5.7 (2201)` at 11:05 (11 min 27 s) and **`1.5.7 (2211)` at 15:42 (8 min 12 s)**, both `LANE EXIT=0` and both independently confirmed `state=VALID` on App Store Connect by a read-only Spaceship query rather than trusted from fastlane's log. The second run is the one that matters — same command, on a machine the first run had already changed — and it was 3¼ min faster, entirely in `build_app` (322 s vs 521 s, incremental IL2CPP). Steps: clean-tree → lock check → Unity batchmode (88 s warm) → `build_app` → `upload_to_testflight` → `mark-uploaded.sh`. **It is not faster than the manual path** — Unity and IL2CPP dominate — it is *walk-away*, and the four dialogs where a human picked the wrong thing are gone. **New:** `Assets/Editor/CIBuild.cs` (profile activated via `BuildProfile.SetActiveBuildProfile`, never `-activeBuildProfile`), `Tools/unity-build-ios.sh`, `Tools/assert-unity-closed.sh`, `Tools/testflight.sh`, `fastlane/Fastfile` + `Appfile` + `.env.example`, runbook § "One command". **Three defects found by running it, not by reading it:** (1) a FAILED batchmode build left `ProjectSettings.asset` dirty — `BuildStampGenerator` restores only on success and its `delayCall` net never gets a frame in batchmode, so the *next* run aborted at `ensure_git_status_clean` blaming a file the pipeline itself dirtied; fixed inside `CIBuild` (snapshot + restore before `Fail()`, since `Exit()` skips `finally`). (2) the first end-to-end attempt died 3 s into `build_app` with `invalid byte sequence in US-ASCII` — gym regexing xcodebuild's `➜` under a US-ASCII Ruby; `LC_ALL` in `fastlane/.env` does **not** fix it (Ruby fixes its external encoding at process start, before dotenv), hence `Tools/testflight.sh` exporting it first. (3) the spec's premise that scheme post-actions don't fire under `xcodebuild` was **wrong** — it fired, and `mark-uploaded.sh`'s idempotency made the double-fire a no-op. **⏭ Cesar:** commit `Docs/Versioning/last_uploaded_build.txt` (now `2211`, the one file a run leaves dirty by design); smoke `2211` on device — it is the first tester build carrying the in-flight `beta_telemetry` code, committed as `bdec09259` to clear the tree for the lane; confirm `In-House Testers` auto-distributed it. **Standing arrangement:** say "punch it" and I preflight (clean tree, Editor closed, guard), run, and confirm at Apple — quitting Unity is pre-authorized, committing your in-flight work is not.

> **▶ 2026-08-18 · `upload_guard_automation` — 🟡 BUILT, needs ONE human check. The App Store build-number regression guard no longer depends on anyone remembering a menu item.** Spec + report: `Docs/Specs/Active/upload_guard_automation/`; STATUS `READY_FOR_ARCHITECT_REVIEW`. **The bug:** `BuildStampGenerator` refuses a store build whose `git rev-list --count HEAD` is ≤ `Docs/Versioning/last_uploaded_build.txt`, but that file was only written by `GOLFIN → Build → Mark Current Commit As Uploaded`. Nobody ran it after the 2026-08-17 `1.5.7 (2192)` upload, so it sat at `0` and the guard had been **inert since the day it was written**. **The fix:** `Tools/mark-uploaded.sh` (writes only on a strict increase, always exits 0, logs every run to the gitignored `Docs/Versioning/.mark-uploaded.log`) + `Assets/Editor/iOSArchivePostAction.cs`, a `[PostProcessBuild(1001)]` that injects an **Xcode Archive post-action** into the generated `.xcscheme`. It has to be injected from Unity because a **Replace** build regenerates the scheme and destroys anything added through Xcode's Edit Scheme UI — same reasoning as the `Info.plist` key in `iOSPostProcess.cs`, which is untouched (zero diff, regression-tested by stripping the key and re-running its callback). **Both spec `NOTE` markers verified, not assumed:** the scheme lives in `xcshareddata/xcschemes/` (checked across `iOS-Full`, `iOS-Dev`, `iOS-Demo`), and `$PROJECT_DIR/../..` is the repo root — but the depth is now **computed** from `pathToBuiltProject`, so a different output path can't silently break it. Idempotent (2 passes → 1 post-action), no-op on Android, and a missing scheme warns instead of failing the build. The exact `scriptText` parsed back out of the scheme was run under archive conditions (`/bin/sh`, `env -i`, stripped `PATH`, cwd `/`) and advanced the guard. **Side effect worth knowing:** the guard file now reads `2192` — the truthful 1.5.7 upload — instead of `0`. **⏭ Needs Cesar:** run **Product → Archive** once and confirm the guard advances `2192 → 2195`. The post-action was injected into the **existing** `Builds/iOS-Full` project, so this needs **no Unity rebuild**. **Not committed** — `TellCode.md`, `ARCHITECTURE_AUDIT.md` and the `fastlane_testflight_pipeline/` spec folder were already dirty from an earlier session, and CLAUDE.md rule 12 halts a close-out on drift outside the task folder.

Migration applied to prod and verified over PostgREST (12 columns by name, anon 401, placement CHECK bites); both halves deployed and a real banner created in the panel renders on device from the live endpoint. **⏭ Open follow-ups:** (1) the Home slot is now 970×214 (aspect **4.53**) after the 24px-gap fix, but the live art is 978×262 (3.73) and the `Image` is Simple with `preserveAspect` off — so it is **stretched**; either author at the slot ratio or turn on `preserveAspect`. `lib/banner.ts` already advertises 970×214 but **the dashboard has not been redeployed** with that guidance. (2) Untested for lack of DATA, not code: JA swap (`image_url_ja` null), tap-through (`link_url` null), `[BannerArt] Cache HIT` across two cold launches, `sort_order` tie-break (one active row).** Spec `Docs/Specs/Active/game_banners/SPEC.md`, report `IMPLEMENTER_REPORT.md`. **Cesar's one step:** paste `playlife/backend/migrations/2026_08_17_game_banners.sql` into the Supabase SQL editor. `public.game_banners` does **not** exist yet (verified over PostgREST: `PGRST205`), so `fly deploy` and `npm run deploy` are deliberately **not** run — deploying code that reads a missing column 500s the endpoint (`ADMIN_DASHBOARD_OPS.md` §3.2). After the paste: verify the column list over PostgREST, then `fly deploy` (playlife), then `npm run deploy` (dashboard).
>
> **What it does.** The Home promo strip (`Canvas/ScreensRoot/HomeScreen/PromoBanner`) and the Rankings banner (`RankingsScreen/ContentArea/Banner`) shipped inside the build and could only change with a store release. Now: a **Banners panel** at `admin.golfin.world/banners` writes `public.game_banners`, a no-auth `GET /api/v1/banners` on playlife-api serves at most one live row per placement (server-side `is_active` + schedule-window + sort selection), and the client swaps the sprite and opens an allowlisted external URL on tap. **The bundled sprites stay in the build and remain the fallback** — no network, an expired row, a refused URL and a failed download all end with exactly the art that ships today. Verified in play mode with the endpoint absent: `[Banners] Banner fetch failed (NotFound, HTTP 404)` → `source=None`, `TryGet=False`, `sprite=GPS Banner`, button non-interactable, **zero Console errors**.
>
> **Reuse, not new systems.** `TournamentArtService` was **parameterized, not forked** — it grows a private `(tag, cacheDirName, isAllowed)` ctor and a second instance `TournamentArtService.Banners` (`[BannerArt]`, `<persistentDataPath>/game-banners`), so the size-capped `DownloadHandlerScript`, `redirectLimit = 0`, atomic cache writes, in-flight coalescing and the LRU sweep are shared rather than duplicated into a second security-critical download path. `TournamentArtPolicy.IsAllowed` split into `IsAllowedUnder(url, allowedRoot)` with every check moved across unchanged; `BannerPolicy.IsArtAllowed` calls it with the `game-banners/` root. `ScheduleRefreshThrottle` reused verbatim (60s), `RemoteTournamentSource` copied in shape, `ApiClient.Get<string>` + `Endpoints`. **Every pre-existing `TournamentArtPolicy`/`TournamentArtService` test still passes unmodified** — EditMode `Golfin.Tournaments.WireupTests` 115 passed / 0 failed, proven to actually execute with a deliberate tripwire (added → run went red on it → removed → green).
>
> 🔒 **Two allowlists, and the client's is the control.** Art: scheme + host + `game-banners/` path prefix compared on the NORMALIZED `Uri` (a raw `StartsWith` passes `…/game-banners/../../../rest/v1/rpc/x`). Links: `https` only, no userinfo, default port, **exact ordinal host match** against `golfin.io`, `www.golfin.io`, `golfin.world`, `www.golfin.world` — no suffix matching, because a `*.golfin.io` rule is precisely what lets `evil-golfin.io` and `golfin.io.attacker.net` through. **⚠️ NEEDS CESAR: confirm that host list.** It ships in the build, so a campaign page on a marketing/Notion/partner host needs a client release — an admin cannot add one from the dashboard, by design. The dashboard runs the same rule as a usability guard; a URL it accepts but the client refuses is a banner that looks fine to the operator and does nothing on the device.
>
> **Trap that was real:** `HomeScreenController.promoBannerButton`, `promoBannerText` and `gpsIcon` were all `{fileID: 0}` — the strip was a dead `Image` with no `Button`, so `OnPromoBannerClicked` had never run in a build. That wiring is NEW, not reused. `promoBannerText`/`gpsIcon` stay unassigned: the content model is image-only, all copy baked into the artwork. Second trap: `RankingsScreenController` is an **added-component override on the ShellScene instance**, not part of `RankingsScreen.prefab` — the Button/binder go in the prefab, the controller's two new fields are wired on the scene instance. **ShellScene diff is 116 insertions / 1 deletion, only the named changes**; four `m_fontSize: 19.85 → 19.95` TMP auto-size churn lines the save baked in were reverted line-by-line and the scene reloaded clean.
>
> **Dashboard verified on fixtures** (isolated `MOCK_MODE=1` server on its own `distDir`, so Cesar's running `next dev` was never touched): panel loads, LIVE/SCHEDULED/EXPIRED/OFF badges, deactivating a LIVE banner requires the typed label (409 without it, 409 on a wrong one, 200 on the right one), activating an art-less row is refused, an off-allowlist link is refused, `.gif` and a 1.9 MB PNG are both refused, re-uploading the same bytes yields the **same** content-hashed URL, unauthenticated `GET` is 401, and all six audit actions (`banner_create/update/delete/activate/deactivate/art_upload`) write rows with before/after. **Still needs a device:** the four manual items in SPEC §6 (remote image on Home, JA language swap, deactivate→relaunch→bundled, `[BannerArt] Cache HIT` on second launch) — none can run until the migration lands and both halves deploy.

> **▶ 2026-08-17 · `tournament_schedule_refresh` — ✅ DONE (Cesar confirmed in the Editor). The schedule now refetches every time the player opens the tournament screen, so an admin edit reaches a running app with no relaunch.** `f66d3b39e`, Quick task, spec folder moved to `Docs/Specs/Quick/Completed/tournament_schedule_refresh/`. `TournamentSelectionScreenController.OnEnable` paints the cached list FIRST (the existing `RebuildNextFrame()` path, untouched) and *then* calls the new `TournamentService.RefreshSchedule()`, which returns on the same frame — no spinner, no gate, nothing waits on the socket, and every failure path leaves the current schedule in place with a single existing `LogWarning`. **No new systems:** the fetch, the atomic cache write, live→cache→CSV precedence, `OnScheduleChanged`, `MergePreservingEntered` and the art prefetch are all Phase 3 reused unchanged, and `Golfin.Net` was **not** added to `Golfin.Tournaments.asmdef` — this lives in `TournamentsRuntime/` like the rest of Phase 3.
>
> **Throttle:** new `ScheduleRefreshThrottle` — in-flight guard + one 60s constant that `TournamentService.ScheduleRefreshCooldownSeconds` *reads* rather than redeclaring. Pure C# with time passed in, so it unit-tests without a MonoBehaviour or a socket. `Awake`'s boot warm now goes through the same entry point, so opening the screen seconds after launch does not double-fetch, and `RefreshScheduleRoutine` is wrapped in `try/finally` so all four `yield break`s release the guard (a stuck guard would silently kill refresh for the whole session). ⚠️ **Deliberate deviation from the spec wording:** the cooldown is armed when an attempt **SETTLES**, not when it succeeds. §3.1 said "the last successful fetch", but success-only arming is the retry storm §3.2 forbids — offline, `UnityWebRequest` fails in milliseconds, so five screen entries would be five requests.
>
> **The Activate/Deactivate switch made "the server no longer sends this tournament" routine, and that exposed TWO holes, not one.** (1) **The list yanking out from under an open modal** — `RebuildCards()` destroys every card including the one the signup modal was opened from; the rebuild is now deferred while `ModalController.OpenModalCount > 0` and flushed on `ModalStackEmptied`. (2) **A dangling id** — deferring the rebuild does *not* stop the backend swapping, so the player can sit on the modal and press CONFIRM. `MergePreservingEntered` correctly does not protect that tournament (no entry yet), and `Backend.GetTournament` **throws `KeyNotFoundException`** — which meant the signup modal's existing `def == null` checks had always been dead code. New `TournamentService.TryGetTournament` returns null instead; the modal toasts and closes rather than dead-ending inside a button handler. Worth remembering as a shape: **a null-check in front of a method that throws is not a guard, it is unreachable code.**
>
> 16 new EditMode tests (`Assets/Scripts/TournamentsRuntime/Tests/ScheduleRefreshTests.cs`) — the throttle, plus entered-tournament survival and the vanished-id guard at the **screen** level, not just the mapper's (this was the B1 bug in the Phase-3 review). Full EditMode swept per assembly across all 17: **1262 total / 1259 passed / 0 failed / 3 pre-existing skips**. `Golfin.TournamentsRuntime.Tests` reported exactly 89 and the file `[Test]` counts are 52+16+21=89, so the new tests provably executed. (The "No tests found" flake hit once again on a first call — retried, ran fine.) ⏭ **Still device-side, non-blocking:** acceptance 1/2/4/6 need a build plus a live admin edit; specifically worth trying is opening the signup modal, deactivating that tournament in the admin, waiting for the refresh and pressing CONFIRM → expect the toast and a clean close.

> **▶ 2026-08-17 · 🌐 THE ADMIN DASHBOARD IS LIVE ON THE INTERNET at `https://admin.golfin.world` — read `Docs/ADMIN_DASHBOARD_OPS.md` before touching it.** Cloudflare Workers (worker `golfin-admin`, OpenNext adapter) in the **Next Innovation** Cloudflare account (`c2c4b98…`, the one that owns the `golfin.world` zone — `account_id` is pinned in `wrangler.jsonc` because that login has two accounts and a Worker can only take a custom domain from a zone in its own). **No `workers.dev` URL, deliberately** — a second unprotected hostname would sit outside the Access policy. **Two independent gates:** Cloudflare Access at the edge (app "GOLFIN Admin", policy "Admins", the two `ADMIN_EMAILS` addresses, team domain `late-cake-f2a4.cloudflareaccess.com`) and then the app's own Supabase login + allowlist — behind both sits a `service_role` key with unrestricted write access to prod, so anything weakening either gate is a production security change. Deploy is `npm run deploy` from `Tools/admin-dashboard` (runs `scripts/cf-deploy.sh`); verify with `curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" https://admin.golfin.world/` — **302 is correct, 200 means Access is not protecting it.** Adding an admin takes **two** edits, `ADMIN_EMAILS` (Worker secret) *and* the Access policy, which fail differently.
>
> **⚠️ The traps, each of which has already cost real time** (full detail + symptoms in the ops doc): (1) **never run `next build` while `next dev` is running** — they share `.next/`, the running server then serves HTML pointing at deleted chunks, every JS/CSS asset 404s, the page loads unstyled and never hydrates, and **the server log stays clean**; this took the dashboard down for a day. (2) **Tooling shells inherit `NODE_ENV=production`** — `npm install` then prunes every devDependency (typescript, tailwind, wrangler) and the build dies with `Can't resolve '@/lib/…'`, while `next dev` compiles middleware in a mode the Edge sandbox rejects and 500s every page with `EvalError: Code generation from strings disallowed`. Use `NODE_ENV=development`. (3) **The `service_role` key must not enter the bundle** — Next loads env files at build time and OpenNext writes them into the uploaded Worker; `cf-deploy.sh` hides the env file for the build, greps the output for the key and **aborts** if found. (4) **…but `NEXT_PUBLIC_*` must** — they are inlined at compile time so a Worker secret cannot supply them; hiding the env file took them too and the app deployed, passed Access, then died on "required in live mode". The local env file is `.env.development.local` (dev-only) precisely so production builds do not see it. (5) **`lib/mode.ts` throws on a missing service key rather than falling back to mock mode**, whose login accepts any password; an earlier version keyed off `NODE_ENV === "production"`, which is not reliably set on Workers and so would never have fired — do not reintroduce environment-name inference. (6) **Migration before deploy, always** — and you cannot run DDL: Supabase's REST API has no DDL path, so write the migration, have Cesar paste it into the SQL editor, and confirm by dumping the live column list before deploying. (7) Shell: `pkill -f "next dev"` from a tooling shell kills its own shell (use `pgrep -f "[n]ext dev"` + `kill`), macOS has no `setsid`, and `fly deploy` outruns the 60s tool timeout — background it and poll.
>

Tournaments=6`. **✅ DONE (Cesar, 2026-08-14)** — spec folder moved to `Docs/Specs/Completed/tournaments_unity_wiring/`. **Acceptance 4 closed with REAL art after the Architect seeded `lomond_championship-8a7161e9de90.png` (260x360, 19158 B):** art cache wiped -> launch 1 logged `[TournamentArt] Downloaded and cached (18 KB)` and wrote `d1974ce81676ad69.png`, matching the SHA-256-derived key exactly with a byte count identical to the server object and no `.tmp` residue -> launch 2 logged `[TournamentArt] Cache HIT (18 KB), no download`, arriving via `WarmArt -> Prefetch` on the **DISK CACHE** boot path, which also proves B6 (prefetch/sweep on every boot path) in production. All three schedule sources have now been observed live: `BUNDLED CSV`, `DISK CACHE`, `SERVER (live fetch)`. >
> **Two bugs the implementation caught that are worth remembering.** (1) **Newtonsoft converts date-shaped strings at the READER level**, before they reach a `string` field — typing `start_at` as `string` is not enough. On a UTC+7 machine `"2026-08-25T00:00:00+00:00"` arrived as `"08/25/2026 07:00:00"`, i.e. every player would have received a schedule shifted by their own timezone. `DateParseHandling.None` on both the reader and the serializer is the fix, and it is why the mapper deliberately does **not** reuse `ApiEnvelope.TryUnwrap` despite sharing its envelope. (2) The existing wire-up guard resolves `Compose` **by reflection with zero args**, so adding an optional parameter throws `TargetParameterCountException` and an overload throws `AmbiguousMatchException` — hence the distinct name `ComposeFrom`. Any future change to a reflection-resolved method has the same trap.
>
> **Architect review (`ARCHITECT_REVIEW.md`, `61829b9aa`) found 6 client blockers before the commit was allowed**, all since fixed and **tripwired** (each headline fix reverted one at a time, matching test went red, restored): an entered tournament that left the server payload was dropped rather than preserved, after which every `GetTournament(id)` threw `KeyNotFoundException` (signup modal, result modal, round handler, `SubmitHoleResult`); the `banner_url` allowlist was a raw-string `StartsWith`, so `…/tournament-art/../../../rest/v1/rpc/x` passed the check and `System.Uri` then normalized the dot segments back out of the bucket — now `Uri`-parsed on `Scheme`/`Host`/`AbsolutePath` with userinfo, non-default port and escaped dot segments all rejected; plus `redirectLimit = 0`, a `Content-Length` refusal before buffering (1 MB), the cache sweep skipping `*.tmp` (it raced the prefetch it was started alongside), and prefetch+sweep running on **every** boot path rather than only after a live fetch. ⚠️ **And the timezone test did not guard the timezone fix** — it passed with `DateParseHandling.None` removed, because Newtonsoft's `RoundtripKind` round trip is character-identical on a UTC host; it would have gone green in CI while the exact regression it existed to catch shipped. Now asserts byte-identity. Spec D3 was also amended: it said "never a merge" while §4.2 required keeping an entered definition, which collide whenever the in-play row came from the CSV — the mid-entry carry-forward is now D3's one named exception.
>
> **🔧 TWO STANDING TOOLING TRAPS, recorded next to "filtered EditMode runs mask failures".** (a) **A stale assembly reads exactly like a real failure.** A probe run without an assets refresh measures the PREVIOUS build — after restoring a tripwired file the probe still reported the fix broken, and the only way to tell that apart from a genuine regression is to force the refresh and re-measure. Refresh before believing any post-restore or post-revert probe. (b) **`tests-run` intermittently returns "No tests found" for a perfectly valid assembly** (it did so for both Tournaments assemblies on one sweep pass and twice for an unfiltered run). It is transient — retry. Do **not** read it as zero tests, and do not read it as a green run.
>
> ⏭ **Open:** acceptance **1** (brand tournament end-to-end) and **3** (airplane-mode cold launch) need a device. The art **download** path is now unblocked — a real 260×360 PNG was uploaded to the public `tournament-art` bucket (`lomond_championship-8a7161e9de90.png`, 19 KB, verified publicly GETtable and carried in the endpoint payload), so the fetch/decode/cache chain has a live object to run against instead of only refusal and 404 paths. ⚠️ Note that object was uploaded by raw `curl`, which does not set `cacheControl` the way supabase-js does, so it serves `cache-control: no-cache` — harmless (the client's own disk cache is keyed by the immutable URL and does not rely on HTTP caching), but a panel-uploaded image will differ. `_placeholderImage` stays deliberately unwired: warn-and-hide beats a wrong photo, and with the dashboard's course dropdown limited to the six ids that all ship art, the branch is a guard against hand-edited SQL rather than an everyday path.

> **▶ 2026-08-14 · `tournaments_unity_wiring` — SHIPPED, both halves. The game reads the schedule from the server, artwork and all.** Server: `playlife ece354f`, deployed, `GET /api/v1/tournaments/golfin` live (six tournaments, 22 bands). Client: committed after an architect review found **six blockers**, all now closed. **The two that mattered:** (1) `PreserveEnteredTournaments` iterated the INCOMING payload, so an entered tournament the server had dropped (deleted or `kind`-flipped) vanished — after which every `GetTournament(id)` throws `KeyNotFoundException` in the signup modal, the result modal, the round handler and `SubmitHoleResult`, i.e. a mid-round hole submission kills the app. It is now a **union**, extracted to a pure tested `TournamentScheduleMapper.MergePreservingEntered`. (2) The `banner_url` allowlist was a raw `StartsWith`, defeated by `…/tournament-art/../../../rest/v1/rpc/x` — `System.Uri` normalizes the dot segments back OUT of the bucket before the request is sent, so scheme and host survived but the path half of the control did not; now `Uri`-parsed `Scheme`/`Host`/normalized `AbsolutePath` + userinfo and port rejection. Plus `redirectLimit = 0` (a 30x would otherwise cache third-party bytes under an allowlisted key, reloading every launch with no network check), a custom `DownloadHandlerScript` refusing on `Content-Length` **before buffering** with a 1 MB cap (`Prefetch` fires unattended at boot, so an oversized object was an OOM on launch), the cache sweep skipping the `*.tmp` files prefetch is mid-write on, and prefetch+sweep now running on **every** boot path so the 50 MB bound exists offline too. **⚠️ Lesson worth keeping: a test can pass while guarding nothing.** The timezone test was character-identical on a UTC host (Newtonsoft's `RoundtripKind` is instant-preserving) — it would have gone green in CI while the exact regression shipped; it now asserts the DTO string is **byte-identical** to the input. All three headline fixes were **tripwired** (reverted one at a time; the matching test went red each time) — and that exercise surfaced a second trap: a post-restore probe read as a failure because Unity had not recompiled, so it was measuring the tripwired build. **Always `assets-refresh` before trusting a `script-execute` probe.** EditMode **1233 total / 1230 passed / 0 failed**, per-assembly sweep reconciling exactly. Acceptance 2/4/6/9 closed in the Editor against the live endpoint — including a live boot logging two real `DEFERRED update` lines from actual save entries, then `Schedule source: SERVER (live fetch)`. Remaining: 1 and 3 need a device, and no tournament has `banner_url` set yet so the art DOWNLOAD half has never run against a real object. `_placeholderImage` deliberately unwired (art call). Report: `Docs/Specs/Active/tournaments_unity_wiring/IMPLEMENTER_REPORT.md`. Original kickoff below.

> **▶ 2026-08-14 · `tournaments_unity_wiring` — iteration-1 summary (superseded by the entry above).** Phase 3 + 3b built in one pass. The client now reads the schedule from the server, artwork and all. Art moved to `Resources/TournamentImages/` (git rename, `.meta` carried, GUIDs preserved); the positional `_courseImages[csvIndex]` fallback is **deleted from the class and from the prefab's serialized data**, and the null-shadowing map bug is fixed. (2) The existing wire-up guard resolves `Compose` by reflection with zero args, so optional params threw `TargetParameterCountException` and an overload threw `AmbiguousMatchException` — hence the distinct name `ComposeFrom`. **⛔ Nothing reaches players until the `playlife` change is deployed** (`/tournaments/golfin` returns HTTP 500 on the live host right now); until then the client sits on the CSV, which is the designed degradation and is what a live play-mode boot logs. Acceptance 1/2/4/6/9 need that deploy plus a device. `_placeholderImage` is intentionally unwired — an art call for Cesar. Report: `Docs/Specs/Active/tournaments_unity_wiring/IMPLEMENTER_REPORT.md`. Original kickoff below.

> **▶ 2026-08-14 · `tournaments_unity_wiring` — original spec/kickoff (superseded by the entry above).** **Cesar's decision of record (2026-08-14):** *"Tournaments names/images are not necessarily tied to a country club. Can be brands as well."* A tournament's identity is **not** its venue — "PUMA Summer Slam" can be played at Lomond with PUMA key art. That reshaped the phase twice over: **(1) remote art ships now, not in a later 3b** (a bundled course photo cannot express a brand, so `banner_url` becomes layer 1 and the course photo demotes to the offline default); **(2) it exposed a real gap** — the card name is `LocalizationManager.Get(def.NameKey)` with **no fallback** (`TournamentSelectionScreenController.cs:153`) and localization keys ship inside the build, so a dashboard-created tournament would render its raw key string; the server therefore sends `title` and the client falls back `localize(name_key) → title → slug`. **Without that, "add a tournament without a new build" was false for its name.** `course_id` keeps exactly one job: which venue is played, and the subtitle line. Other decisions of record in the spec: **do NOT add `Golfin.Net` to `Golfin.Tournaments.asmdef`** — that assembly is deliberately dependency-light, and the fetch/mapping/art service go in `Assets/Scripts/TournamentsRuntime/` (no asmdef → Assembly-CSharp, which already sees `Golfin.Net` and `Golfin.Economy` via `autoReferenced`); the shipped CSV stays the offline fallback and a no-network cold launch must behave **exactly as today**; server data replaces CSV data **wholesale or not at all** (a half-server schedule is unreproducible in a bug report); state stays client-derived via `DeriveState`; and bad server data (dangling `bot_field_id`, empty ladder) **drops that tournament with an error**, never silently. Art: move `Art/Tournaments/CourseImages/` → `Resources/TournamentImages/` (already named by `course_id`, `Resources.Load` is extension-agnostic, no renames), **delete** the positional `_courseImages[csvIndex]` fallback (`:31`, `:333-334`) and fix the null-shadowing bug at `:330` where a map entry with a null Sprite blanks the card instead of falling through; disk cache under `persistentDataPath/tournament-art/` keyed by URL hash, 50 MB LRU, prefetched on the schedule fetch. 🔒 The client accepts a `banner_url` only under the exact Storage host **and** path prefix — `/tournaments/admin/create` still writes these tables behind a static key, so the column is not exclusively the dashboard's. Server half (playlife): new no-auth `GET /api/v1/tournaments/golfin` (kind='golfin', bands joined, no status filter) plus a `kind` filter on `/tournaments/active` (`:61`) and on `auto_enter_score`'s two selects (`:239`, `:248`) — today a real-world GPS score can enter a game tournament. ⚠️ Standing caveat this phase does NOT fix: only `lomond-country-club` has playable hole data, `HoleParProviderAdapter.cs:35-37` ignores `clubId`, and the two id namespaces (`lomond` vs `lomond-country-club`) never meet — a tournament on Kawana still plays Lomond's holes. Spec: `Docs/Specs/Active/tournaments_unity_wiring/SPEC.md`; kickoff archived in `Docs/TellCode.md`.
>
> **▶ 2026-08-14 · `tournaments_server_side` — PHASES 1–2 SHIPPED. The tournament schedule now lives in Postgres and has an admin panel.** Phase 1 (migration `playlife/backend/migrations/2026_08_13_tournaments_golfin.sql`, **APPLIED to prod**, playlife `02fb177`): `tournaments` gains `kind` ('gps'|'golfin'), `slug`, `name_key`, `course_id`, `hole_set`, `bot_field_id`, `resolve_delay_minutes`, `league_key`, `bot_seed`, a `tournaments_golfin_complete` CHECK so a golfin row cannot be half-defined, and a partial unique index on `slug`; new `tournament_prize_bands` (per-tournament, **not** reusable `prizeTableId` templates — raising one tournament's first prize must not silently change three others) and `tournament_hole_results`; `tournament_entries` extended with `character_id`, `holes_completed`, `status`, `is_bot`, `display_name`, `submitted_at` for async multiplayer. The 6 shipped fixtures + 22 prize bands seeded verbatim, so the server starts as a faithful mirror of the CSV. **Two migration traps recorded:** `ON CONFLICT (slug)` cannot infer a PARTIAL unique index (42P10 — needs the `where slug is not null` predicate spelled out), and `tournaments_tier_check` only allows `open|member|champion`, so DIAMOND/GOLD/SILVER lives in `league_key` and every golfin row is `tier='open'`. Phase 2 (GolfinRedux `0e5c509d0`): **Tournaments panel** in `Tools/admin-dashboard` — list with state DERIVED from `start_at`/`end_at` by the same rule as `LocalTournamentBackend.DeriveState` (⚠️ `status` is deliberately NOT maintained for golfin rows; two sources of truth for "is it open" is how schedules rot), rank-band prize editor validating gaps/overlaps/missing-rank-1/the 2000 RP `tournament_prize` cap, duplicate + delete-with-cascade-count, a loud typed-slug confirmation when the tournament is Open or Ending, read-only entries tab, artwork upload to a public `tournament-art` Storage bucket (created on first use; type/size validated, aspect warned against the shipped 260×360 card, immutable content-hashed names so the URL is its own cache key, `banner_url` host-validated server-side), and everything audited through `lib/audit.ts`. **`course_id` is a DROPDOWN, never free text** — the panel must not be able to author a tournament the game cannot draw. ⚠️ **There is no course registry in the Unity project**: `courseId` is a free-form string that `CheckReferentialIntegrity` does not check (it validates only `prizeTableId` and `botFieldId`); the six ids are a convention held together by three unlinked files — `tournaments.csv`, `LocalizationText.csv:190` (`tourn.venue.<id>`), and `Art/Tournaments/CourseImages/<id>` — now written down in `lib/courses.ts`. Only `lomond-country-club` has playable hole data, and the two id namespaces (`lomond` vs `lomond-country-club`) never meet. **Honest limitation, stated in a persistent banner in the panel: edits do NOT reach players yet** — the game still reads the shipped CSV, so until Phase 3 the panel is the authoring tool and ships via "Export tournaments.csv" / "Export tournament_prizes.csv" → drop into `Assets/Resources/Data` → commit → build. ⏭ **Phase 3 (Unity, Claude Code, needs its own kickoff):** read the schedule from the server, move `Art/Tournaments/CourseImages/` under `Resources/TournamentImages/` and resolve by `course_id`, and **DELETE the positional `_courseImages[csvIndex]` fallback** in `TournamentSelectionScreenController.ResolveSprite:323` — it is the bug, not a safety net (reordering silently reshuffles which photo lands on which card). Then 3b remote art + 4–5 entries/submission/leaderboard/resolver. Spec: `Docs/Specs/Active/tournaments_server_side/SPEC.md`.

> **▶ 2026-08-14 · `rp_balance_sync` — CODE COMPLETE, waiting only on Cesar's manual pass.** The inbound half of the Slice-2 cutover: Slice 2 taught the game to WRITE to the ledger and never to read it back, so the nav-bar RP counter showed a stale local number forever and dashboard grants were invisible in game. Built: `RewardPointsManager.ApplyServerBalance` (the one authorized inbound writer — deliberately NOT behind `AllowLocalOverride`, since that guard refusing while the flag is ON *was* the bug), an `IServerBalanceSink` / `ServerBalanceSync` seam in `Golfin.Economy`, and `ServerBalanceSyncBehaviour` in Assembly-CSharp that refreshes on **sign-in / app foreground / entering Home / startup-if-already-signed-in** — event-driven, no polling. Displayed RP is `server balance + queued earns`, so a fresh earn never flickers away while the queue drains, and nothing is pushed at all until the server has answered once (`HasBalance` separates "0 RP" from "unknown"). **New `AuthService.SignedIn` static event** — no sign-in hook existed; that is the one API added outside the economy stack. **One flagged ordering fix:** `PointsService.SpendRoutine` now folds the post-debit server total in AFTER `onDone`, because doing it before would make the newly-live wire double-debit the local counter. EditMode **1190 passed / 0 failed / 3 pre-existing skips of 1193**, run per-assembly across all 17 EditMode assemblies. **Cesar's remaining step (SPEC §5.3, the real proof):** flag ON, signed in as `cesar.guarinoni@gmail.com` (live balance **173**) → nav bar reads 173 → grant **+25** in the dashboard → background/foreground → **198, no restart**. > **🔧 THREE PROJECT-WIDE FIXES · 2026-08-11 (Cesar-directed, alongside `tournaments_mode_card`).**
>
> **1. Tournament fixtures refreshed — the harness runs end-to-end again.** Every row in `Assets/Resources/Data/tournaments.csv` still carried its original 2026-06/07 dates, so by August **all six tournaments had genuinely ended**: the T7 screen correctly showed a wall of ENDED cards with no `SIGN UP` CTA anywhere, and `TournamentLoopCaptureHarness` therefore could not get past its signup step. **That was the data being out of date, not a defect** — the schedule was doing its job. Fix is simply fresh absolute dates (last refreshed 2026-08-11), giving Open ×3 / Upcoming ×1 / Ended ×2 now, with `lomond` opening Aug 18 and `hirono` closing Aug 14. `Ending` is by nature the final hour before `endUtc`, so no row sits in it permanently — each Open row passes through it on the way out, which is correct behaviour rather than a gap. Row order IS render order (no sort) and the harness clicks the FIRST `SIGN UP`, so **keep an Open row at the top (`kasumigaseki`)**. Result: harness reached `=== TournamentRoundLoop: SEQUENCE COMPLETE ===` (signup → hole 1 par 5 → hole 2 par 4 → leaderboard). EditMode suite 1113 passed / 0 failed.
>
> ⚠️ **A clock-relative date form (`now+5d`) was tried here and REVERTED — do not reintroduce it.** It made the harness pass, but `tournaments.csv` ships (it is under `Resources/`, and `LocalTournamentBackend` is the only backend), so anchoring to session load time gave every player a private schedule: the same tournament "ending in 4d" forever, never actually starting or finishing, and two players in different windows sharing one leaderboard. **Every build must show the same tournaments at the same times**, which is exactly what absolute dates in the CSV already deliver. The CSV is the intended source of tournament detail; the only maintenance it needs is refreshing the dates when a cycle expires. `TournamentCsvLoader.ParseUtc` is absolute-only again, with a comment recording why.
>
> **2. `DynamicFontAtlasGuard` — dynamic TMP atlases no longer pollute the diff.** `Assets/Scripts/Editor/DynamicFontAtlasGuard.cs` clears baked glyphs from **Dynamic** font assets on `EnteredEditMode` (not `ExitingPlayMode` — the runtime is still alive then and re-bakes). Rendering Japanese once grew `NotoSansJP-VariableFont_wght SDF.asset` 59KB → **2.27MB**; it had to be hand-reverted twice in one session. Toggle: `GOLFIN > Fonts > Clear Dynamic Atlases On Play Exit` (on by default), plus `Clear Dynamic Atlases Now`. **Two non-obvious findings, both measured:** `setAtlasSizeToZero: true` does NOT zero `atlasWidth/Height` (they stay 1024×1024) — it drops the 1MB Alpha8 texture payload, and `TryAddCharacters` afterwards still rasterises normally, so nothing ships as tofu; passing `false` leaves the pixels serialized and does not shrink the file **at all** (2,104,924 bytes either way), defeating the purpose. All dynamic fonts are now committed in the guard's steady state, and the clear is **idempotent** (two passes byte-identical), so future play sessions round-trip to exactly the committed bytes. Static/pre-baked atlases are never touched.
>
> **3. `build_bot_video.py` caption gaps.** The ffmpeg caption font has no CJK and no `→` — both render as tofu boxes. Keep caption text ASCII; the UI itself carries the localized strings. Also `--caption-fontsize` / `--caption-wrap` were added because the default sizes from height (`h/32` = 79px) and overflows a 1170-wide portrait clip off both edges.

> **🔧 PIPELINE FIX (affects EVERY Figma-node UI task, not just this one) — `enforce_implementer_done.py` Rule 21 was permanently blocking STATUS transitions.** `_rerun_ui_lint_via_editor()` (~line 1903) built its C# inside a non-raw f-string, so the emitted script never compiled: `\"fail\":` collapsed to `""fail":` (CS1003/CS0103) and `\s`/`\d` leaked into the C# literal (CS1009). It therefore always returned `None`, and since iter-3 made the check **fail-CLOSED**, no Figma-node task could advance. The editor was never unreachable — `_call_live_editor()` returned a live test string from `http://localhost:21573/mcp` throughout, and manual `LintPrefab` runs returned `fail: 0`. **Fixed 2026-08-11 on Cesar's instruction, 2 lines:** `StartsWith("\\"fail\\":")` and a regex emitting `"\"fail\"\\s*:\\s*(\\d+)"`. Verified end-to-end: emitted C# compiles, live re-run returns `fail = 0` for both prefabs, a non-zero verdict still propagates (`LINT_FAIL_COUNT:3` → `3`), and unreachable-editor / prefab-not-found still return `None` so the gate stays fail-closed. Hook suite **118 passed, 1 failed**, that one failure (`TestLiveEditorIntegration::test_real_clone_matches`) reproducing identically on unmodified HEAD. ⚠️ **If any other task was stuck at `IMPLEMENTER_BLOCKED` on a "could not obtain a FRESH linter run" message, it can now advance.**
